`CHANGELOG.md`:

```md
# Changelog

All notable changes to this project will be documented in this file.

The format is based on [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.0.0/),
and this project adheres to [Semantic Versioning](https://semver.org/spec/v2.0.0.html).

## [Unreleased]

### Added
- Versioned ARM64 research artifacts with build manifests, payload checksums and
  archive/installation integrity checks.
- Native ARM64 artifact CI, repeat-build comparison and draft GitHub releases.
- `owlbeard --version` and kernel module metadata report the release build identity.

### Changed
- CI validates Terraform without AWS access. AWS provisioning and remote builds
  are manual operations, separate from research artifact verification.
- Commit hooks check staged file hygiene and credentials only; full test suites
  and artifact verification run in CI.

## [2.6.0] - 2026-03-15

### Added
- `daemon/log.h`: header-only logging macro system with 4 levels (ERR/WARN/INFO/DBG), runtime filtering via `g_owl_log_level`, compile-time DBG gate via `OWL_DEBUG`. ERR/WARN route to stderr, INFO/DBG to stdout. Mirrors kernel module `pr_info`/`pr_warn`/`pr_err` pattern.
- `tests/test_log.c`: 7 unit tests — output routing, level filtering, prefix injection, format interpolation, compile-time DBG elimination
- `--verbose`/`-v` and `--quiet`/`-q` CLI flags for daemon log level control

### Changed
- `daemon/main.c`: migrated ~35 printf/fprintf/perror call sites to OWL_ERR/OWL_WARN/OWL_INFO macros, added `g_owl_log_level` global and `--verbose`/`--quiet` flag parsing
- `daemon/bpf_loader.c`: migrated 15 call sites to log macros, enhanced libbpf callback to respect `g_owl_log_level` for LIBBPF_DEBUG
- `daemon/self_protect.c`: migrated 5 call sites to log macros
- `daemon/integrity.c`: migrated 1 call site to OWL_INFO
- `daemon/vdso_integrity.c`: migrated 1 call site to OWL_INFO
- `tests/Makefile`: added `test_log` to TEST_BINS (18 suites total)

### Fixed
- `daemon/self_protect.c`: dual-logging bug — `owl_selfprotect_watchdog()` wrote "[ALERT] kernel module unloaded!" to both stderr and stdout with explicit flushes. Now single OWL_WARN call to stderr only.
- Alert messages (`[ALERT] ...`) that were incorrectly routed to stdout now go to stderr via OWL_WARN

## [2.5.0] - 2026-03-15

### Added
- WP4: Speed hack detection via multi-clock drift and vDSO integrity
- `daemon/clock_validator.{h,c}`: CLOCK_MONOTONIC vs CLOCK_MONOTONIC_RAW drift detection with 50ms threshold; ARM64 CNTVCT_EL0 cross-check via inline `mrs` instructions
- `daemon/vdso_integrity.{h,c}`: HMAC-SHA256 baseline/check of [vdso] mapping pages; detects runtime vDSO patching (speed hacks bypassing LD_PRELOAD)
- `OWL_EVENT_CLOCK_DRIFT` (0x0700), `OWL_EVENT_VDSO_TAMPER` (0x0701) event types
- `cheats/speed_hook_so.c`: LD_PRELOAD .so that intercepts `clock_gettime(CLOCK_MONOTONIC)` and returns 2x elapsed time
- `cheats/speed_hack.c`: launcher that sets `LD_PRELOAD=speed_hook.so` and execs game
- `tests/test_clock_validator.c`: 7 unit tests (TDD) — drift computation, init, null handling
- `tests/test_vdso_integrity.c`: 5 unit tests (TDD) — maps parsing, init, buffer tamper detection
- 2 E2E assertions in `scripts/verify.sh` for speed_hack baseline + protected phases

### Changed
- `include/owlbear_events.h`: added 0x0700 range comment, CLOCK_DRIFT and VDSO_TAMPER event types
- `daemon/main.c`: clock_validator and vdso_integrity includes, init, event_loop integration (clock check 5s watchdog, vDSO check 30s periodic), event_type_str/print_event cases, policy rules
- `daemon/Makefile`: added `clock_validator.c`, `vdso_integrity.c` to SRCS
- `tests/Makefile`: added `test_clock_validator`, `test_vdso_integrity` test suites
- `cheats/Makefile`: added `speed_hack.c` to CHEAT_SRCS, `speed_hook.so` build rule with `-ldl`
- `scripts/verify.sh`: version bumped to v2.5.0, added speed_hack to preflight + E2E phases

## [2.4.0] - 2026-03-15

### Added
- WP3: Network monitoring via eBPF kprobes (`ebpf/owlbear_net.bpf.c`)
- kprobes on `tcp_v4_connect`, `udp_sendmsg`; filter by protected PID; observe-only
- `OWL_EVENT_NET_CONNECT` (0x0600), `OWL_EVENT_NET_SEND` (0x0601) event types
- `struct owl_payload_network`: dst_addr, dst_port, protocol, bytes, comm
- `daemon/net_allowlist.{h,c}`: static IP allowlist, logs if destination not in list
- `cheats/net_exfil.c`: UDP game state exfiltration cheat
- `tests/test_net_allowlist.c`: 8 unit tests
- 2 BPF conversion tests for network events in `test_bpf_loader.c`

### Changed
- `include/owlbear_events.h`: network event types + payload + union member
- `daemon/bpf_loader.c`: loads owlbear_net skeleton
- `daemon/bpf_event_convert.c`: NET_CONNECT/NET_SEND conversion
- `daemon/event_pipeline.{h,c}`: allowlist integration, updated init signature
- `daemon/main.c`: network event formatting, policy rules, allowlist init

## [2.3.1] - 2026-03-15

### Fixed
- ARM64 ptrace syscall injection: replaced `PTRACE_SINGLESTEP` with `PTRACE_SYSCALL` enter/exit pair for executing injected SVC instructions. `PTRACE_SINGLESTEP` over `SVC #0` on kernel 6.17 ARM64 does not execute the syscall — registers are unchanged after the step. x86_64 path unchanged.
- `read_result()` in `mprotect_inject_via_ptrace.c`: added error check on `PTRACE_GETREGSET` return (previously returned uninitialized stack data on failure)
- mmap return value check: reject 0 as failure (`<= 0` instead of `< 0`), since 0 means the syscall never ran
- LD_PRELOAD detection race condition: `owl_check_preload_env()` now retries up to 3 times with 50ms backoff when `/proc/<pid>/environ` is unreadable. The exec tracepoint fires during `execve()` processing before the new process image is fully set up.

## [2.3.0] - 2026-03-15

### Added
- **WP2: HMAC-SHA256 code integrity** (`daemon/hmac_sha256.c`): replaces CRC32 with HMAC-SHA256 using per-session 256-bit random key from `/dev/urandom`. Uses OpenSSL one-shot `HMAC()` (not deprecated `HMAC_CTX_*`). Graviton3 ARMv8.4 SHA extensions accelerate computation.
- `daemon/hmac_sha256.h`: public API (`owl_hmac_sha256`, `owl_hmac_generate_key`)
- `tests/test_hmac_sha256.c`: 10 unit tests (TDD) — RFC 4231 known-answer vectors, empty/large input, key change, null inputs, key generation, integrity baseline+check, violation detection
- Buffer-based integrity functions (`owl_integrity_baseline_buffer`, `owl_integrity_check_buffer`) for testable verification without /proc I/O
- `scripts/verify.sh`: HMAC-SHA256 baseline assertion in protected phase, ld_preload_hook baseline assertion

### Changed
- `daemon/integrity.h`: `baseline_crc` replaced with `baseline_hmac[32]` + `hmac_key[32]` in `struct owl_integrity`
- `daemon/integrity.c`: baseline and check functions use HMAC-SHA256 via buffer variants; CRC32 retained for heartbeat `state_hash`
- `daemon/Makefile`: added `hmac_sha256.c`, linked `-lssl -lcrypto`
- `tests/Makefile`: added `test_hmac_sha256` suite, updated `test_integrity` linking with OpenSSL
- `tests/test_integrity.c`: init test checks HMAC fields instead of CRC32
- `scripts/verify.sh`: version bumped to v2.3.0
- `README.md`: updated status (v2.3.0, 125 tests, 14 suites), HMAC-SHA256 in daemon description, removed CRC32 limitation
- `deploy/terraform/modules/graviton-dev/userdata.sh`: added `libssl-dev` to build dependencies
- `.github/workflows/ci.yml`: install `libssl-dev` on Graviton before build (OpenSSL headers required for HMAC-SHA256)

## [2.2.0] - 2026-03-15

### Added
- **WP1e: Process tree construction from tracepoint events** (`daemon/process_tree.c`): open-addressing hash map (linear probing, 1024 slots) storing PID -> {parent_pid, comm, birth_time}. Fed by fork/exec/exit events via `owl_ptree_on_event()` in the pipeline. Provides `owl_ptree_is_descendant()` and `owl_ptree_get_chain()` for correlation engine ancestry queries.
- `daemon/process_tree.h`: public API (init, destroy, insert, remove, lookup, is_descendant, get_chain, on_event)
- `tests/test_process_tree.c`: 8 unit tests (TDD) — insert/lookup, parent chain, ancestry chain, descendant check, remove, capacity, null inputs, reinsert after exit

### Changed
- `daemon/event_pipeline.h`: added `struct owl_ptree *ptree` field to pipeline context, added ptree parameter to `owl_pipeline_init()`
- `daemon/event_pipeline.c`: calls `owl_ptree_on_event()` after LD_PRELOAD check for process lifecycle events
- `daemon/main.c`: instantiates `struct owl_ptree`, passes to pipeline init, destroys on cleanup
- `daemon/Makefile`: added `process_tree.c` to SRCS
- `tests/Makefile`: added `test_process_tree` suite, linked `process_tree.o` into `test_event_pipeline`
- `tests/test_event_pipeline.c`: updated `owl_pipeline_init()` calls with NULL ptree parameter

## [2.1.0] - 2026-03-15

### Added
- **WP1d: LD_PRELOAD detection on process exec** (`daemon/preload_detect.c`): scans `/proc/<pid>/environ` for `LD_PRELOAD` on every `PROCESS_EXEC` event. Emits `OWL_EVENT_LIB_UNEXPECTED` (0x0203, severity CRITICAL) with the preload path in the module payload. Pure scanner function + I/O wrapper, stateless one-shot per exec.
- `daemon/preload_detect.h`: public API (`owl_scan_environ_for_preload`, `owl_check_preload_env`)
- `tests/test_preload_detect.c`: 5 unit tests (TDD) — synthetic buffer scan, not-found, null buffer, null output, self-check via /proc
- `scripts/verify.sh`: E2E assertion for `ld_preload_hook` triggering `LIB_UNEXPECTED` in daemon log

### Changed
- `daemon/event_pipeline.c`: calls `pipeline_check_preload()` on `OWL_EVENT_PROCESS_EXEC` events, emits `LIB_UNEXPECTED` if `LD_PRELOAD` found
- `daemon/Makefile`: added `preload_detect.c` to SRCS
- `tests/Makefile`: added `test_preload_detect` suite, linked `preload_detect.o` into `test_event_pipeline`
- `scripts/verify.sh`: version bumped to v2.1.0, added `ld_preload_hook.bin` to preflight checks
- `README.md`: updated status (v2.1.0, 107 tests, 12 suites), added LD_PRELOAD detection

## [2.0.0] - 2026-03-15

### Added
- **WP1c: Block /dev/mem and /dev/kmem via eBPF LSM** (`ebpf/owlbear_lsm.bpf.c`): extends `file_open` hook to unconditionally block `/dev/mem` and `/dev/kmem` opens with -EPERM. Physical memory access bypasses all process-level protections, so no PID allowlist check is performed. Emits `OWL_EVENT_DEV_MEM_ACCESS` (severity CRITICAL, target_pid=0). Detail string distinguishes `/dev/mem` vs `/dev/kmem`.
- `OWL_EVENT_DEV_MEM_ACCESS` (0x0106) in shared event header and BPF common header (breaking: new event type)
- `cheats/dev_mem_reader.c`: attack program that attempts to open `/dev/mem` and `/dev/kmem` and read 256 bytes of raw physical memory
- `tests/test_bpf_loader.c`: conversion test for `OWL_EVENT_DEV_MEM_ACCESS` (102 total tests)
- `scripts/verify.sh`: baseline and protected phase assertions for dev_mem_reader, handles CONFIG_STRICT_DEVMEM (EACCES) gracefully

### Changed
- `daemon/bpf_event_convert.c`: `OWL_EVENT_DEV_MEM_ACCESS` falls through to memory payload conversion
- `daemon/main.c`: `event_type_str()` and `print_event()` handle `DEV_MEM_ACCESS`
- `cheats/Makefile`: builds `dev_mem_reader.bin`
- `scripts/verify.sh`: version bumped to v2.0.0
- `README.md`: updated status (v2.0.0, 102 tests, 9 attack programs), added /dev/mem blocking

## [1.2.0] - 2026-03-15

### Added
- **WP1b: TracerPid polling for debugger detection** (`daemon/debugger_detect.c`): polls `/proc/<pid>/status` for non-zero `TracerPid` every 5s in the watchdog loop. Detects debuggers that attached before daemon start or via methods eBPF hooks don't cover. Emits `OWL_EVENT_PTRACE_ATTEMPT` with `source=OWL_SRC_DAEMON` on 0-to-nonzero state transition. 6 unit tests (TDD).

### Changed
- `scripts/verify.sh`: version bumped to v1.2.0
- `README.md`: updated status (101 tests, 11 suites), added TracerPid mention, removed stale "no anti-debug" limitation

## [1.1.0] - 2026-03-15

### Added
- **WP1a: In-process mprotect injection cheat** (`cheats/mprotect_inject_via_ptrace.c`): realistic code injection attack chain — PTRACE_ATTACH to game, inject mmap+mprotect syscalls into game's execution context via register manipulation and single-stepping. The mprotect(RW->RX) call originates from the game's PID, correctly firing the eBPF LSM `file_mprotect` hook. Supports ARM64 (SVC #0) and x86_64 (syscall). Replaces the skipped E2E assertion with 2 real detection checks.

### Changed
- `scripts/verify.sh`: added `mprotect_inject_via_ptrace` to baseline (assert injection succeeds) and protected (assert PTRACE_ATTEMPT or MPROTECT_EXEC detection) phases. Replaced `assert_skip` for mprotect detection with real assertions. Version bumped to v1.1.0.
- `cheats/Makefile`: builds `mprotect_inject_via_ptrace.bin`

## [1.0.0] - 2026-03-14

### Added
- **eBPF Loading and Enforcement (WP1):**
  - `daemon/bpf_loader.c`: loads/attaches all three BPF skeletons (LSM, tracepoint, kprobe), populates `protected_pids`/`allowed_pids` maps, polls ring buffer via `ring_buffer__poll()`
  - `daemon/bpf_event_convert.c`: pure BPF event → owlbear_event conversion (testable without libbpf)
  - `ebpf/owlbear_lsm.bpf.c`: new `SEC("lsm/file_mprotect")` hook detects RW→RX transitions in protected process
  - `OWL_EVENT_MPROTECT_EXEC` (0x0105) added to events header and BPF common header
  - Graceful degradation: if any BPF program fails, others still operate; kmod-only fallback
- **Event Pipeline (WP2):**
  - `daemon/event_pipeline.c`: unified event processing — policy evaluation → enforcement action → logging. `[ENFORCE] [BLOCK]` log entries when `--enforce` is passed. SIGKILL on `OWL_ACT_KILL`. Periodic signature scan on game `.text` via `/proc/<pid>/maps` + `/proc/<pid>/mem`
  - `daemon/sig_loader.c`: parses `signatures/default.sigs` line format (`NAME:PATTERN`), handles comments and malformed lines
  - Default policy rules: BLOCK on ptrace/proc_mem/vm_readv/vm_writev in enforce mode; LOG on signature matches and module events
- **New Cheat Vectors (WP3):**
  - `cheats/ptrace_writer.c`: PTRACE_ATTACH + PTRACE_POKEDATA to write `health=9999`
  - `cheats/vm_writer.c`: `process_vm_writev()` to write `health=9999`
  - `cheats/mprotect_injector.c`: `mmap(RW)` → write ARM64/x86 shellcode → `mprotect(RX)` → execute
- **Code Integrity (WP4):**
  - `daemon/integrity.c`: CRC32 hash of game `.text` segment at baseline, periodic re-verification. Parses `/proc/<pid>/maps` for `r-xp` segments, emits `OWL_EVENT_CODE_INTEGRITY_FAIL` on mismatch
- **Self-Protection (WP5):**
  - `daemon/self_protect.c`: `prctl(PR_SET_DUMPABLE, 0)` blocks ptrace on daemon. Watchdog checks `/sys/module/owlbear/` existence, ioctl responsiveness, BPF ring buffer fd liveness every 5s
  - `kernel/owlbear_integrity.c`: kprobe on `delete_module` emits `OWL_EVENT_MODULE_UNKNOWN` on any module unload
- **Daemon epoll multiplexer:** replaced blocking `read()` loop with `epoll` on both `/dev/owlbear` fd and BPF ring buffer fd, periodic timer for integrity check (30s) and self-protection watchdog (5s)
- **New tests:** 5 suites (test_bpf_loader, test_sig_loader, test_event_pipeline, test_integrity, test_self_protect) adding 38 tests. Total: 95 C tests across 10 suites
- **verify.sh v2:** three phases (baseline, protected, self-protection), tests all 8 cheats, checks EPERM enforcement from eBPF LSM, integrity baseline capture, module unload detection, daemon survival

### Changed
- `daemon/main.c`: full rewrite — integrates BPF loader, event pipeline, integrity, self-protection, epoll event loop. Adds `--sigs` CLI flag
- `daemon/Makefile`: builds all new source files, links `-lbpf -lelf -lz`, adds `-I../ebpf` for skeleton headers
- `cheats/Makefile`: builds 3 new cheat binaries (ptrace_writer, vm_writer, mprotect_injector)
- `tests/Makefile`: builds 5 new test suites with daemon object dependencies
- `.github/workflows/verify.yml`: E2E verification on Graviton3 via SSM — finds instance by tag, starts if stopped, syncs code, builds eBPF+kernel+userspace, runs unit tests + `verify.sh --upload`
- `deploy/terraform/bootstrap/main.tf`: added `ssm:SendCommand`, `ssm:GetCommandInvocation`, `ssm:DescribeInstanceInformation`, `ec2:StartInstances` permissions for GitHub Actions role
- `deploy/terraform/modules/graviton-dev/userdata.sh`: added `make -C ebpf` to instance bootstrap (generates skeleton headers needed by daemon)

## [0.9.0] - 2026-03-14

### Added
- E2E verification script (`scripts/verify.sh`): adversarial evidence package builder
  - Two-phase design: baseline (no module, cheats succeed) vs protected (module loaded, detection fires)
  - Per-cheat artifact capture: strace log, stdout/stderr, exit code, dmesg diff
  - Assertions on exact `pr_warn` strings from kprobe handlers with correct PIDs
  - PAC false positive regression check across full check interval
  - kprobes list snapshot at module load
  - SHA-256 manifest of all artifacts for tamper detection
  - Self-contained timestamped output directory (`/tmp/owlbear-verify-YYYYMMDD-HHMMSS/`)
  - Machine-generated `summary.txt` with PASS/FAIL/SKIP per assertion — no LLM in the loop
  - `--upload` flag: syncs evidence package to S3 (`owlbear-verification-artifacts` bucket)
  - `--bucket` / `--region` overrides; auto-detects region via IMDSv2
  - EC2 instance identity document captured for provenance (instance ID, account ID)
  - `latest.txt` pointer in S3 for easy retrieval of most recent run
- S3 verification artifacts bucket (`owlbear-verification-artifacts`) in graviton-dev Terraform module
  - Versioning enabled, AES-256 SSE, all public access blocked
  - 90-day lifecycle expiration, 30-day noncurrent version cleanup
  - IAM policy on Graviton instance profile: PutObject, GetObject, ListBucket

## [0.8.0] - 2026-03-14

### Fixed
- ARM64 `extract_vm_rw_target` double-dereference: kernel 6.1+ passes user pt_regs directly, `regs->regs[0]` IS the first syscall arg. Removed invalid pointer cast that caused EFAULT/EIO in cheats
- PAC key false positive: workqueue reads kworker's per-process PAC keys, not the game's. Removed key value comparison, kept only `SCTLR_EL1.EnIA` global enable check
- Demo script address parsing: replaced fragile stderr+sed with info file read
- `strtoull` base 16 hardcoded in cheats: changed to base 0 (auto-detect `0x` prefix)

### Added
- Game info file (`/tmp/owlbear-game.info`): game writes `PID 0xADDR`, unlinks on exit. Shared `GAME_INFO_FILE` constant in `game_state.h`
- Info file fallback in `mem_reader`, `proc_mem_reader`, `ptrace_injector`: run with no args to auto-discover game PID and address
- `ptrace_injector`: real implementation using PTRACE_ATTACH + PTRACE_PEEKDATA word-by-word read, prints stolen state, detaches
- `-no-pie` in game CFLAGS/LDFLAGS for stable addresses across runs
- `test_info_parse` (8 tests): valid format, missing prefix, large address, trailing whitespace, garbage, negative PID, empty file, missing file
- Total test count: 65 (57 C across 5 suites + 11 Python, up from 60)

### Changed
- Demo script uses info file for PID/address discovery with log-parsing fallback
- Demo script runs `ptrace_injector` as cheat #3

## [0.7.0] - 2026-03-13

### Added
- End-to-end demo script (`scripts/demo.sh`): builds, loads module, starts game + daemon, runs each test cheat, shows detection events, cleans up on exit. Handles ARM64 and x86 gracefully.
- GitHub Pages site (`docs/`): Jekyll with minima theme
  - Architecture overview: data flow diagram, why-hybrid rationale, event pipeline, communication table
  - Process monitoring: tracepoint implementation, ptrace kprobe + BPF LSM, defense-in-depth
  - Memory protection: attack vectors, kprobe targets, BPF LSM file_open dentry walk, non-fatal registration
  - ARM64 hardware checks: system register monitoring, debug register scanning, PAC key verification, periodic workqueue trade-offs
  - PAC/MTE/BTI explainer: what each does, anti-cheat applications, hardware availability, x86 comparison
  - ARM64 vs x86: privilege model, memory architecture, I/D cache coherency implications, debug capabilities, kernel patch protection
- GitHub Pages deployment workflow (`.github/workflows/pages.yml`)
- Total test count unchanged: 60 (49 C + 11 Python)

## [0.6.0] - 2026-03-13

### Added
- Terraform bootstrap: S3 state bucket, DynamoDB lock table, GitHub Actions OIDC provider + IAM role with scoped permissions for catalyst-dev (516608939870)
- Graviton dev module: c7g.large (Graviton3, PAC-capable), Amazon Linux 2023 ARM64, SSM Session Manager access, auto-shutdown via EventBridge, userdata installs build deps and clones repo
- Telemetry platform module: API Gateway HTTP API, Lambda (Python 3.12, arm64), DynamoDB tables (events + heartbeats) with TTL, SSM-stored API key, CloudWatch log group

## [0.5.0] - 2026-03-13

### Added
- Signature scanner (`daemon/scanner.c`): hex byte-pattern matching with `??` wildcards, compiled pattern database, linear scan
- Signature scanner tests (17 tests, TDD): pattern parsing, matching, database operations
- Heartbeat tracker (`daemon/heartbeat.c`): frame count anomaly detection (freeze/rewind), timeout with configurable threshold
- Heartbeat tracker tests (10 tests, TDD): registration, processing, anomalies, timeouts
- Lambda telemetry receiver (`platform/api/handler.py`): POST /events (batched), POST /heartbeat (returns action), GET /events (dashboard query), GET /health
- Lambda handler tests (11 tests): routing, auth (Bearer token), input validation, DynamoDB mocking
- Signature database (`signatures/default.sigs`): 5 rules targeting test cheat string signatures
- Total test count: 60 (49 C across 4 suites + 11 Python)

## [0.4.0] - 2026-03-13

### Added
- eBPF LSM programs: `ptrace_access_check` (deny + log), `file_open` (/proc/pid/mem deny + log), `mmap_file` (PROT_EXEC monitor)
- eBPF tracepoint programs: `sched_process_exec`, `sys_enter_process_vm_readv`, `sys_enter_process_vm_writev`
- eBPF kprobe: `do_init_module` for kernel module load detection
- BPF maps: `protected_pids` (hash), `allowed_pids` (hash), `events` (1MB ringbuf), `event_count` (percpu)
- Shared BPF header (`owlbear_common.bpf.h`): maps, helpers, event struct for ring buffer
- Policy engine (`daemon/policy.c`): rule-based event-type + severity -> action mapping, first-match-wins, wildcard event type support
- Policy engine tests (9 tests, TDD): init defaults, exact match, severity threshold, wildcard, first-match, capacity, action strings, custom default
- Test cheat: `ld_preload_hook` launcher + `preload_hook.so` (replaces damage function for god mode)

## [0.3.0] - 2026-03-13

### Added
- ARM64 system register monitoring: SCTLR_EL1, TCR_EL1, MAIR_EL1, MDSCR_EL1, VBAR_EL1 baseline snapshot at init, periodic re-verification via delayed workqueue (5s interval)
- WXN disable detection: reports if Write-implies-eXecute-Never is cleared (enables W+X pages)
- VBAR_EL1 redirect detection: reports if exception vector table base changes
- MDSCR_EL1 KDE detection: reports if kernel debug is enabled post-init
- Hardware debug register scanning: reads DBGBCR/DBGBVR 0-5 and DBGWCR/DBGWVR 0-3, reports any active breakpoints/watchpoints
- PAC key integrity: captures APIAKeyHi/Lo_EL1 at init, detects substitution (conditional on CONFIG_ARM64_PTR_AUTH + hardware support)
- PAC SCTLR_EL1.EnIA monitoring: detects if pointer authentication is disabled
- Kernel module enumeration: walks loaded module list, reports modules loaded after owlbear
- Non-ARM64 stub: module loads on x86 with ARM64 checks disabled (cross-compile testing)
- Test cheat: `debug_reg_setter` — ptrace attach + PTRACE_SETREGSET NT_ARM_HW_BREAK on ARM64
- Register ID enum and ARM64 bit definitions in `owlbear_arm64.h`

## [0.2.0] - 2026-03-13

### Added
- SPSC lock-free ring buffer with `smp_wmb`/`smp_rmb` barriers for kernel-to-userspace event delivery
- `/dev/owlbear` chardev: blocking read, poll/epoll, ioctl (SET_TARGET, CLEAR_TARGET, GET_STATUS, SET_MODE)
- Process monitoring via `sched_process_fork/exec/exit` tracepoints and `__ptrace_may_access` kprobe
- Memory protection kprobes: `mem_open`, `__arm64_sys_process_vm_readv/writev`, `vm_mmap_pgoff` (PROT_EXEC), `do_init_module`
- Userspace daemon with blocking event loop, structured logging, CLI target/enforce/log configuration
- ncurses test game: mutable game state with health/armor/position/aim, function pointer hook targets
- Test cheats: `mem_reader` (process_vm_readv), `proc_mem_reader` (/proc/pid/mem)
- 13 unit tests: struct sizes, alignment, field offsets, event type ranges, ioctl uniqueness

## [0.1.0] - 2026-03-13

`Makefile`:

```
# Owlbear Anti-Cheat — Top-Level Build Orchestrator
#
# Targets:
#   all           Build everything (kernel, ebpf, daemon, game, cheats)
#   kernel        Build the kernel module
#   ebpf          Build eBPF programs
#   daemon        Build the userspace daemon
#   game          Build the test game
#   cheats        Build all test cheat programs
#   test          Run unit tests (userspace only, no root required)
#   integration   Run integration tests (requires root + ARM64)
#   clean         Remove all build artifacts
#   help          Show this help
#
# Variables:
#   ARCH           Target architecture (default: detected)
#   CROSS_COMPILE  Cross-compiler prefix (e.g., aarch64-linux-gnu-)
#   KDIR           Kernel source/headers directory
#   V              Verbose build (V=1)
#   DEBUG          Debug build with sanitizers (DEBUG=1)

.PHONY: all kernel ebpf daemon game cheats test integration clean help artifact artifact-test

# Detect architecture
ARCH ?= $(shell uname -m)
ifeq ($(ARCH),aarch64)
  ARCH := arm64
endif

# Kernel headers location
KDIR ?= /lib/modules/$(shell uname -r)/build

# Compiler settings
CC       ?= gcc
CLANG    ?= clang
LLC      ?= llc
BPFTOOL  ?= bpftool

# Shared include directory
INCLUDES := -I$(CURDIR)/include

# Export for sub-makes
export ARCH CROSS_COMPILE KDIR CC CLANG LLC BPFTOOL INCLUDES

# Debug mode
ifdef DEBUG
  export CFLAGS_EXTRA := -g -O0 -fsanitize=address,undefined -DOWL_DEBUG=1
else
  export CFLAGS_EXTRA := -O2 -DNDEBUG
endif

# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Top-level targets
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------

all: kernel ebpf daemon game cheats
	@echo ""
	@echo "=== Owlbear build complete ==="
	@echo "  Kernel module:  kernel/owlbear.ko"
	@echo "  Daemon:         daemon/owlbeard"
	@echo "  Game:           game/owlbear-game"
	@echo "  Cheats:         cheats/*.bin"
	@echo ""

kernel:
	@echo "=== Building kernel module ==="
	$(MAKE) -C $(KDIR) M=$(CURDIR)/kernel modules

ebpf:
	@echo "=== Building eBPF programs ==="
	$(MAKE) -C ebpf

daemon:
	@echo "=== Building daemon ==="
	$(MAKE) -C daemon

game:
	@echo "=== Building test game ==="
	$(MAKE) -C game

cheats:
	@echo "=== Building test cheats ==="
	$(MAKE) -C cheats

# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Testing
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------

test:
	@echo "=== Running unit tests ==="
	$(MAKE) -C tests unit

integration:
	@echo "=== Running integration tests (requires root + ARM64) ==="
	$(MAKE) -C tests integration

# Release builds use committed sources in a temporary tree, never this checkout.
artifact:
	python3 scripts/research-artifact.py build --output dist

artifact-test:
	python3 -m unittest discover -s tests -p 'test_research_artifact.py' -v

# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Cleanup
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------

clean:
	@echo "=== Cleaning all build artifacts ==="
	-$(MAKE) -C $(KDIR) M=$(CURDIR)/kernel clean 2>/dev/null || true
	-$(MAKE) -C ebpf clean 2>/dev/null || true
	-$(MAKE) -C daemon clean 2>/dev/null || true
	-$(MAKE) -C game clean 2>/dev/null || true
	-$(MAKE) -C cheats clean 2>/dev/null || true
	-$(MAKE) -C tests clean 2>/dev/null || true

# -------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Help
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------

help:
	@echo "Owlbear Anti-Cheat Build System"
	@echo ""
	@echo "Usage: make [target] [VAR=value ...]"
	@echo ""
	@echo "Targets:"
	@echo "  all           Build everything"
	@echo "  kernel        Build kernel module (owlbear.ko)"
	@echo "  ebpf          Build eBPF programs"
	@echo "  daemon        Build userspace daemon (owlbeard)"
	@echo "  game          Build test game application"
	@echo "  cheats        Build test cheat programs"
	@echo "  test          Run unit tests"
	@echo "  integration   Run integration tests (root + ARM64)"
	@echo "  artifact      Build a versioned ARM64 archive from HEAD (no root)"
	@echo "  artifact-test Test packaging and integrity checks (any architecture)"
	@echo "  clean         Remove all build artifacts"
	@echo "  help          Show this help"
	@echo ""
	@echo "Variables:"
	@echo "  ARCH=arm64              Target architecture"
	@echo "  CROSS_COMPILE=prefix-   Cross-compiler prefix"
	@echo "  KDIR=/path/to/kernel    Kernel headers directory"
	@echo "  DEBUG=1                 Enable debug build"
	@echo "  V=1                     Verbose output"

```

`README.md`:

```md
# Owlbear

Anti-cheat and EDR prototype. ARM64 Linux, kernel module + eBPF + userspace daemon. Built to learn how kernel-level security monitoring works by building one.

Not production software.

## What's here

Runs on Graviton3 (c7g.large), Ubuntu 24.04, kernel 6.17.

- **kernel/** - loadable module. Kprobes on ptrace, /proc/pid/mem, process_vm_readv/writev, mmap, module load/unload. ARM64 system register monitoring. Chardev for event delivery.
- **ebpf/** - BPF LSM hooks returning -EPERM (ptrace_access_check, file_open for /proc/pid/mem + /dev/mem + /dev/kmem, file_mprotect). Tracepoints. Kprobe on do_init_module. Ring buffer to userspace.
- **daemon/** - epoll on chardev + BPF ring buffer. Policy engine. Signature scanner. HMAC-SHA256 code integrity. Self-protection watchdog. TracerPid debugger detection. LD_PRELOAD environ scanning. Process ancestry tree for correlation.
- **game/** - ncurses test target. Mutable state, function pointers, exported address.
- **cheats/** - 9 attack programs: process_vm_readv, /proc/pid/mem, /dev/mem, ptrace read, ptrace write, process_vm_writev, LD_PRELOAD, mprotect injection, debug registers.
- **platform/** - Lambda + API Gateway + DynamoDB telemetry receiver.
- **scripts/verify.sh** - E2E test. Baseline (cheats succeed) vs protected (cheats blocked). Machine-generated results.

## Status

v2.3.0. 125 unit tests, 14 suites. eBPF LSM returns EPERM on ptrace, /proc/pid/mem, /dev/mem, /dev/kmem, process_vm_writev. Module can't be unloaded while daemon runs. TracerPid polling detects debuggers attached before daemon start. LD_PRELOAD detection on exec. Process tree tracks ancestry for correlation engine.

Prototype limitations: linear signature scan, no fleet management.

## Getting started

### Existing instance

Pre-provisioned Graviton3 in us-east-2 (catalyst-dev). Already built.

```bash
./scripts/connect-graviton.sh       # SSM session

# On the instance:
cd ~/owlbear
sudo scripts/verify.sh              # E2E, ~2 min
```

Interactive:

```bash
# T1: game
./game/owlbear-game

# T2: protection
sudo insmod kernel/owlbear.ko target_pid=$(pidof owlbear-game)
sudo ./daemon/owlbeard --target $(pidof owlbear-game) --enforce

# T3: cheats (get blocked)
./cheats/ptrace_writer.bin           # EPERM
./cheats/proc_mem_reader.bin         # EPERM
./cheats/mem_reader.bin              # detected
```

### New instance

Provisioning is a manual operation using an authorized local AWS profile.
See [manual AWS deployment](deploy/terraform/README.md) for plan review and apply.

```bash
cd deploy/terraform/environments/dev
terraform init
terraform plan
terraform apply
```

Userdata installs deps, clones, builds. ~5 min.

### Local ARM64

```bash
./scripts/setup-dev.sh
make -C ebpf
make all
make test
sudo scripts/verify.sh
```

## What you can learn

- Kernel kprobes intercepting syscalls and internal functions
- eBPF LSM returning -EPERM from BPF programs
- BPF CO-RE compilation, skeleton loading, attachment
- Chardev + ring buffer + epoll event delivery
- ARM64 system register monitoring (SCTLR_EL1, MDSCR_EL1, VBAR_EL1)
- PAC and debug register scanning on Graviton3
- Why anti-cheat and EDR use the same kernel infrastructure
- Why eBPF LSM is replacing custom kernel drivers

## Anti-cheat vs EDR

Same plumbing. Kprobes + eBPF LSM is how CrowdStrike, SentinelOne, and Cilium Tetragon work. Anti-cheat protects one process from everything else. EDR protects the system from specific threats. This prototype sits in the overlap.

Experiment controllers: [versioned control and telemetry interfaces](docs/experiment-interface.md).

BigRAE integration: [independent runtime adapter](adapters/bigrae/README.md).

## Docs

Commit hooks follow the usual pre-commit setup (`pre-commit install`) and only
check staged file hygiene and credentials. Full unit suites, ARM64 builds and
artifact reproducibility checks run in CI. Runtime experiment qualification is
performed separately on the admitted study environment.

[Versioned ARM64 research builds](docs/research-artifacts.md): pinned artifacts,
build manifests, checksums and inspection without running an experiment.

[GitHub Pages site](https://brad-edwards.github.io/owlbear/)

## License

[LICENSE](LICENSE)

`adapters/bigrae/Dockerfile`:

# Supply an approved digest-pinned Python >=3.12 image; no default floating tag.
ARG PYTHON_IMAGE
FROM ${PYTHON_IMAGE}
COPY wheelhouse/ /wheelhouse/
# requirements.lock pins and hashes every transitive wheel, including this adapter.
RUN python -m pip install --no-cache-dir --no-index --find-links=/wheelhouse \
    --require-hashes -r /wheelhouse/requirements.lock
USER 65532:65532
ENV PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1
ENTRYPOINT ["python", "-I", "-B", "-m", "bigrae_adapter_sdk.worker"]

`adapters/bigrae/README.md`:

```md
# Owlbear BigRAE runtime adapter

Independent wheel `owlbear-bigrae-adapter==0.1.0`, entry point `owlbear` in
`bigrae.runtime.plugins`, using `bigrae-adapter-sdk[runtime]==0.1.0`.
The worker returns SDK plans; BigRAE authorizes targets and executes guest actions.
An installation inspection or successful plan does not prove guest readiness.

## Build and test

Build with `uv build --wheel adapters/bigrae`. Install the SDK and adapter wheels
in a separate environment, then run its Python against `adapters/bigrae/tests`.
Do not add the BigRAE checkout to PYTHONPATH or copy platform code into the worker.

## Complete worker delivery

The adapter consumes the SDK's **published CI distribution**, the route supported
by the upstream SDK for independent adapters. `sdk.json` records the upstream
repository, artifact ID, run, revision, ZIP hash and wheel hash. The exact 33 KB
MIT-licensed SDK wheel is preserved under `vendor/`, including its license, so
expiration of an upstream CI download cannot change or break this release.
There is no dependency on an unpublished PyPI coordinate or a mutable checkout.

```sh
python3 adapters/bigrae/release.py --output /absolute/new/release-directory --guest-integration
```

The complete integration run requires native ARM64, Docker, `skopeo` and `uv`. Omit
`--guest-integration` for local worker tests on x86; CI runs the full ARM64 path.
The script verifies the SDK hash, builds the adapter wheel, resolves the exact
versions in `constraints.txt` into a hash-locked wheelhouse for Python 3.12, and
builds the worker from the pinned multi-architecture Python image. The Dockerfile
installs all wheels offline using `--require-hashes`.

A disposable **loopback-only local registry** provides the worker's real registry
manifest digest. The script pushes and pulls that exact identity, tests it under
BigRAE's restrictions, and verifies the exported archive contains the same manifest
and intact blobs. No external registry, cloud provider or BigRAE tenant is used.
The registry container is removed even after a test failure.

The release directory and the CI artifact `owlbear-bigrae-worker-arm64` contain:

- `worker.tar`: standard containers/image directory transport archive, with its registry manifest preserved.
- `image.json`: registry digest, image identity, architecture, base image and SDK identity.
- SDK, adapter and dependency wheels, plus `requirements.lock` and `SHA256SUMS`.
- `worker-integration.json` and `guest-integration.json`: authoritative test receipts.
- `integration-installation.json`: the exact SDK installation declaration tested.

The installation declaration's loopback address belongs to the disposable test
registry. To install later, extract the archive and use `skopeo copy --all --preserve-digests
dir:/extracted/image docker://AUTHORIZED_REGISTRY/REPOSITORY:VERSION` without
changing its manifest and generate the declaration with that registry and the
recorded digest. The registry name may change; the digest must match `image.json`:

```sh
owlbear-bigrae-manifest --worker-image 'AUTHORIZED_REGISTRY/REPOSITORY@sha256:RECORDED_DIGEST'
```

This is artifact delivery and integration qualification, not live-tenant deployment.
The worker runs with UID/GID 65532, no network, no capabilities, read-only root,
bounded memory/PIDs and a bounded `/tmp` tmpfs. All five SDK phases execute from
the actual digest; the test also checks that platform modules are absent.

The separate ARM64 guest test executes complete generated configure/verify scripts
against the real daemon linked to the repository's explicit fake ioctl/BPF fixture.
Its module-loader fixture writes a build note into **private tmpfs sysfs**, never
host sysfs. The test container alone enables SYS_PTRACE and removes AppArmor's
sysfs restriction for this fake filesystem. It loads no real kernel module.
Configuration, idempotent retry, verification, wrong-release and wrong-enforcement
readback, and artifact tampering are checked. This fixture cannot enter the worker
image or adapter wheel. Live sensor/attack qualification remains issue #34 and is
outside these integration tests.

## Configuration

Bind exactly one Linux node as `guest`. Provide one `configuration` parameter,
a JSON string with these exact fields (replace all illustrative identities):

```json
{
  "release": "/opt/owlbear/releases/release-directory",
  "manifest_sha256": "64 lowercase SHA-256 hex characters",
  "build_id": "64 lowercase SHA-256 hex characters",
  "version": "2.5.0",
  "source_commit": "40 lowercase git commit hex characters",
  "kernel_release": "6.17.0-exact-guest-kernel",
  "target_pid": 123,
  "sensors": ["lsm", "trace", "kprobe", "net"],
  "enforce": true
}
```

Configuration **selects** a pre-staged extracted release; it performs no downloads
or transfers. All release files and ancestors must be root-owned and not writable
by others. Use a trusted manifest hash from the intended release. The guest needs
ARM64 Linux, the exact kernel, matching libraries, Python 3, kmod (`insmod`),
binutils (`readelf`) and root guest execution. The isolated worker remains unprivileged.
The caller starts and owns the target process. No attack or game behavior is built
in. Empty `sensors` disables BPF groups; kernel and daemon checks remain active.
Parameters are encoded as JSON data, never interpolated into shell commands.

## Behavior and evidence

`validate` checks the declaration, Linux binding and configuration with no actions.
`configure` authenticates the verifier against the pinned manifest, invokes the
existing artifact verifier, checks embedded build identity and guest kernel, loads
the exact module if absent, then starts the daemon with the declared runtime
parameters. A module already loaded is accepted only if its GNU build note matches
the selected ELF. Guests without readable module build notes fail explicitly.

`verify` repeats identity verification and reads the control socket with a two-second
timeout and bounded output. It checks realized PID, attachments and enforcement,
all three daemon initialization results (signature loading, code-integrity and vDSO
baselines), output health and zero known kernel drops. Unknown BPF loss remains
unknown. The running daemon executable must resolve to the selected release.
Readiness polling has a 15-second deadline; guest actions have a 120-second host
limit and subprocess deadlines. Successful execution prints
`owlbear.bigrae.readiness/v1` with the build identity and actual health response.
The host must use guest exit status, not plan status, as readiness evidence.

State is private to `/run/owlbear-<operation UUID>`, mode 0700. Retries inspect
existing state without replacing processes, sockets or evidence. Incomplete or
incompatible state fails for inspection. Core guest destruction owns cleanup;
the adapter returns no cleanup actions and contains no infrastructure lifecycle,
cloud access, study behavior or evidence upload. Live qualification is issue #34.

```

typing-inspection==0.4.4

```

`adapters/bigrae/integration/Dockerfile`:

```
ARG PYTHON_IMAGE
FROM ${PYTHON_IMAGE}
RUN apt-get update -qq && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends gcc libc6-dev libssl-dev binutils
COPY wheelhouse /wheelhouse
RUN python -m pip install --no-index --find-links=/wheelhouse --require-hashes -r /wheelhouse/requirements.lock
COPY source /source
ENTRYPOINT ["python", "/source/integration/guest_test.py"]

```

`adapters/bigrae/integration/guest_test.py`:

```py
"""Integration only: real guest scripts and daemon, fake module/device and BPF.

Runs in an isolated ARM64 container. /sys is a private tmpfs, never host sysfs.
No real module is loaded. No BigRAE tenant or provider is contacted.
"""
import ctypes
import hashlib
import importlib.util
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import platform
import struct
import subprocess
import sys
import uuid

from bigrae_adapter_sdk.runtime import RuntimeInput, parse_result
from owlbear_bigrae import manifest

source = Path('/source')
spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('artifact', source / 'scripts/research-artifact.py')
artifact = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(artifact)


def main():
    if platform.machine() != 'aarch64':
        raise SystemExit('guest integration requires native ARM64; not a live tenant')
    # Allow the child daemon to baseline this fixture target under Yama.
    if ctypes.CDLL(None).prctl(0x59616d61, ctypes.c_ulong(-1), 0, 0, 0):
        raise RuntimeError('fixture ptrace authorization failed')
    root = Path('/opt/owlbear/releases/integration')
    root.mkdir(parents=True)
    identity = dict(schema='owlbear.build/v1', version='0.1.0', source_commit='a'*40,
                    source_date_epoch=1, architecture='arm64', kernel_release=platform.release())
    identity['build_id'] = artifact.digest(artifact.encoded({'build': identity, 'inputs': {}}))
    header = Path('/source/generated')
    header.mkdir()
    (header / 'owlbear_build.h').write_text('#define OWL_BUILD_INFO_JSON ' + json.dumps(json.dumps(identity)) + '\n')
    daemon = root / 'daemon/owlbeard'
    daemon.parent.mkdir()
    names = ['main', 'experiment', 'event_pipeline', 'process_tree', 'sig_loader', 'integrity',
             'hmac_sha256', 'self_protect', 'debugger_detect', 'preload_detect', 'clock_validator',
             'vdso_integrity', 'policy', 'scanner', 'net_allowlist']
    subprocess.run(['gcc','-std=c11','-D_GNU_SOURCE','-O1', '-I'+str(header),'-I/source/include',
                    '-I/source/daemon', *[str(source/'daemon'/f'{name}.c') for name in names],
                    '/source/tests/experiment_fixture.c','-lcrypto','-o',str(daemon)], check=True)
    module = root / 'kernel/owlbear.ko'
    module.parent.mkdir()
    subprocess.run(['gcc','-shared','-Wl,--build-id','-x','c','-','-o',str(module)],
                   input='int fixture(void) { return 0; }', text=True, check=True)
    for name in artifact.REQUIRED:
        path = root / name
        if path.exists():
            continue
        path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        data = b'fixture\n'
        if name.endswith(('.o','.ko','.so','.bin')) or name == 'game/owlbear-game':
            data = bytearray(64)
            data[:6] = b'\x7fELF\x02\x01'
            data[18:20] = (247 if name.endswith('.bpf.o') else 183).to_bytes(2,'little')
        if name in ('scripts/research-artifact.py','signatures/default.sigs'):
            data = (source / name).read_bytes()
        path.write_bytes(data)
        path.chmod(0o644)
    recorded = {name: dict(sha256=artifact.digest((root/name).read_bytes()),
                          size=(root/name).stat().st_size, mode=(root/name).stat().st_mode & 0o7777)
                for name in artifact.REQUIRED}
    metadata = dict(schema=artifact.SCHEMA,build=identity,inputs={},files=recorded)
    (root/'manifest.json').write_bytes(artifact.encoded(metadata))
    # Explicit module-loader fixture: save the authenticated ELF note in PRIVATE sysfs.
    loader = Path('/usr/local/bin/insmod')
    loader.write_text('''#!/usr/local/bin/python
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess,sys
note=Path('/sys/module/owlbear/notes/.note.gnu.build-id')
note.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
subprocess.run(['objcopy','--dump-section','.note.gnu.build-id='+str(note),sys.argv[1],'/run/fixture-module-copy'],check=True)
''')
    loader.chmod(0o755)
    os.mkfifo('/dev/owlbear',0o600)
    device = os.open('/dev/owlbear',os.O_RDWR | os.O_NONBLOCK)
    config = dict(release=str(root),manifest_sha256=artifact.digest((root/'manifest.json').read_bytes()),
                  **{key: identity[key] for key in ('version','build_id','source_commit','kernel_release')},
                  target_pid=os.getpid(),sensors=[],enforce=True)
    operation = uuid.uuid4()
    selection = manifest('registry.integration.test/owlbear/worker@sha256:'+'a'*64)
    # This process executes the guest plan; the production worker is independently
    # tested from its actual registry digest under the SDK container restrictions.
    def execute(phase, values=config, success=True):
        request=RuntimeInput(protocol=selection.protocol,invocation_id=uuid.uuid4(),operation_id=operation,
            pack_digest='sha256:'+'b'*64,manifest=selection,phase=phase,provider='aws',range_id=1,
            targets={'guest':dict(node_address='nodes.integration',os_family='linux')},
            parameters={'configuration':json.dumps(values)})
        environment=dict(os.environ,BIGRAE_PLUGIN_INPUT=request.model_dump_json())
        worker=subprocess.run([sys.executable,'-I','-m','bigrae_adapter_sdk.worker'],env=environment,
                              capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=10,check=True)
        plan=parse_result(worker.stdout,request)
        if plan.status != 'planned':
            raise RuntimeError('worker did not plan guest action')
        action=plan.actions[0]
        result=subprocess.run(['sh','-s'],input=action.script,text=True,capture_output=True,
                              timeout=action.timeout_seconds)
        if (result.returncode == 0) != success:
            raise RuntimeError(f'guest {phase}: unexpected exit {result.returncode}: {result.stderr}')
        if success:
            receipt=json.loads(result.stdout)
            if receipt['build'] != identity or receipt['health']['kernel_enforce'] is not values['enforce']:
                raise RuntimeError('guest readback did not preserve identity or parameters')
            return receipt
    receipt=execute('configure')
    pid=receipt['health']['pid']
    try:
        if execute('configure')['health']['pid'] != pid:
            raise RuntimeError('retry replaced the daemon')
        execute('verify')
        execute('verify',dict(config,build_id='f'*64),success=False)
        execute('verify',dict(config,enforce=False),success=False)
        signature=root/'signatures/default.sigs'
        signature.write_text(signature.read_text()+'\n# tampered\n')
        execute('verify',success=False)
        print(json.dumps(dict(schema='owlbear.bigrae.guest-integration/v1',ok=True,
            architecture=platform.machine(),checks=['configure','retry','verify','wrong-release',
            'wrong-enforcement','tampered-artifact'],kernel='explicit FIFO/ioctl/module-note fixture',
            live_tenant=False)))
    finally:
        os.kill(pid,15)
        os.close(device)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

```

`adapters/bigrae/integration/worker_test.py`:

```py
"""Run the actual registry-pinned worker under BigRAE's isolation constraints."""
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import sys
from uuid import uuid4

from bigrae_adapter_sdk.runtime import RuntimeInput, parse_input, parse_result
from owlbear_bigrae import manifest

image, receipt_path=sys.argv[1:]
selection=manifest(image)
config=dict(release='/opt/owlbear/releases/integration',manifest_sha256='a'*64,build_id='b'*64,
            version='0.1.0',source_commit='c'*40,kernel_release='6.17.0-integration',target_pid=123,
            sensors=['lsm'],enforce=True)
flags=['docker','run','--rm','--network','none','--read-only','--cap-drop','ALL',
       '--security-opt','no-new-privileges','--pids-limit','64','--memory','256m',
       '--tmpfs','/tmp:rw,noexec,nosuid,size=16m']
checks=[]
for phase in ('inspect','validate','configure','verify','cleanup'):
    request=RuntimeInput(protocol=selection.protocol,invocation_id=uuid4(),operation_id=uuid4(),
        pack_digest='sha256:'+'d'*64,manifest=selection,phase='verify',provider='aws',range_id=1,
        targets={'guest':dict(node_address='nodes.integration',os_family='linux')},
        parameters={'configuration':json.dumps(config)})
    data=request.model_dump(mode='json')
    data['phase']=phase
    if phase == 'inspect':
        data={k:data[k] for k in ('protocol','invocation_id','manifest','phase')}
    original=parse_input(json.dumps(data))
    result=subprocess.run([*flags,'--env','BIGRAE_PLUGIN_INPUT',image],
        env=dict(os.environ,BIGRAE_PLUGIN_INPUT=original.model_dump_json()),
        capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=30,check=True)
    parsed=parse_result(result.stdout,original)
    if parsed.status != ('compatible' if phase == 'inspect' else 'planned'):
        raise RuntimeError('worker did not complete '+phase)
    checks.append(phase)
# Verify the package cannot import platform modules and the effective UID is fixed.
probe="import os,importlib.util; assert os.getuid()==65532; assert importlib.util.find_spec('shifter_platform') is None; assert not os.access('/',os.W_OK)"
subprocess.run([*flags,'--entrypoint','python',image,'-I','-c',probe],check=True,timeout=30)
Path(receipt_path).write_text(json.dumps(dict(schema='owlbear.bigrae.worker-integration/v1',
    worker_image=image,ok=True,checks=checks,uid=65532,network='none',root_filesystem='read-only',live_tenant=False),indent=2)+'\n')

`adapters/bigrae/release.py`:

```py
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Build and integration-test a relocatable worker release. Never publish externally."""
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tarfile
import time
from uuid import uuid4
from zipfile import ZipFile

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parents[1]
BASE = 'python:3.12-slim@sha256:aa943ce411423c7582288f18707ff66eddcbaeb510502d747a72aeb34f30d159'
REGISTRY = 'registry:3@sha256:d4032d4cc323c1731c707931ecada07a53076eac38e3b61ae57533f170f2c6c3'


def run(args, **kwargs):
    return subprocess.run([str(arg) for arg in args],check=True,timeout=600,**kwargs)


def output(args):
    return run(args,stdout=subprocess.PIPE,text=True).stdout.strip()


def sha(path):
    return hashlib.file_digest(path.open('rb'),'sha256').hexdigest()


def write(path, value):
    path.write_text(json.dumps(value,sort_keys=True,indent=2)+'\n')


def build(destination, guest):
    destination=destination.resolve()
    destination.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=False)
    wheelhouse=destination/'wheelhouse'
    wheelhouse.mkdir()
    sdk=json.loads((HERE/'sdk.json').read_text())
    sdk_wheel=HERE/'vendor'/sdk['wheel']
    if sha(sdk_wheel) != sdk['wheel_sha256']:
        raise ValueError('SDK distribution differs from the published candidate')
    shutil.copy2(sdk_wheel,wheelhouse/sdk_wheel.name)
    # Build only this project; the SDK is consumed as the published wheel, not rebuilt.
    run(['uv','build','--wheel','--out-dir',wheelhouse,HERE])
    shutil.copy2(HERE/'constraints.txt',wheelhouse/'constraints.txt')
    wheels=sorted('/wheelhouse/'+p.name for p in wheelhouse.glob('*.whl'))
    run(['docker','run','--rm','--user',f'{os.getuid()}:{os.getgid()}',
         '--mount',f'type=bind,src={wheelhouse},dst=/wheelhouse',BASE,
         'python','-m','pip','download','--no-cache-dir','--only-binary=:all:',
         '--constraint','/wheelhouse/constraints.txt','--dest','/wheelhouse',*wheels])
    requirements=[]
    for wheel in sorted(wheelhouse.glob('*.whl')):
        with ZipFile(wheel) as archive:
            metadata=archive.read(next(n for n in archive.namelist() if n.endswith('.dist-info/METADATA'))).decode()
        fields=dict(line.split(': ',1) for line in metadata.splitlines() if line.startswith(('Name: ','Version: ')))
        requirements.append(f"{fields['Name']}=={fields['Version']} --hash=sha256:{sha(wheel)}")
    (wheelhouse/'requirements.lock').write_text('\n'.join(requirements)+'\n')
    shutil.copy2(HERE/'Dockerfile',destination/'Dockerfile')
    image='owlbear-integration-'+uuid4().hex
    registry='owlbear-registry-'+uuid4().hex
    run(['docker','build','--build-arg','PYTHON_IMAGE='+BASE,'--tag',image,destination])
    image_id=output(['docker','image','inspect',image,'--format','{{.Id}}'])
    # A loopback-only disposable registry exercises a REAL registry manifest digest.
    # It has no tenant credentials and receives only this local test image.
    run(['docker','run','--detach','--name',registry,'--publish','127.0.0.1::5000',REGISTRY],stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL)
    try:
        port=output(['docker','port',registry,'5000/tcp']).rsplit(':',1)[1]
        tagged=f'127.0.0.1:{port}/owlbear-worker:integration'
        run(['docker','tag',image,tagged])
        for attempt in range(10):
            try:
                run(['docker','push',tagged])
                break
            except subprocess.CalledProcessError:
                if attempt == 9:
                    raise
                time.sleep(.5)
        repository_digest=next(value for value in json.loads(output(
            ['docker','image','inspect',tagged,'--format','{{json .RepoDigests}}']))
            if value.startswith(tagged.rsplit(':',1)[0]+'@'))
        digest=repository_digest.rsplit('@',1)[1]
        run(['docker','pull',repository_digest])
        # Docker's classic `save` rewrites/discards the registry manifest. Use
        # the standard containers/image directory transport to preserve its bytes.
        image_directory=destination/'image'
        run(['skopeo','copy','--all','--preserve-digests','--src-tls-verify=false',
             'docker://'+repository_digest,'dir:'+str(image_directory)])
        if sha(image_directory/'manifest.json') != digest.removeprefix('sha256:'):
            raise ValueError('exported image does not preserve the registry manifest')
        with tarfile.open(destination/'worker.tar','w') as archive:
            archive.add(image_directory,arcname='.')
        # The release's runner installs only wheels, never application source.
        environment=destination/'test-runtime'
        run(['uv','venv','--python','3.12',environment])
        python=environment/'bin/python'
        run(['uv','pip','install','--python',python,'--no-index','--find-links',wheelhouse,
             '--require-hashes','--requirements',wheelhouse/'requirements.lock'])
        selection=json.loads(output([environment/'bin/owlbear-bigrae-manifest','--worker-image',repository_digest]))
        write(destination/'integration-installation.json',selection)
        run([python,HERE/'integration/worker_test.py',repository_digest,destination/'worker-integration.json'])
        run([python,'-I','-m','unittest','discover','-s',HERE/'tests','-v'])
        if guest:
            context=destination/'guest-context'
            context.mkdir()
            shutil.copytree(wheelhouse,context/'wheelhouse')
            for path in ['daemon','include','signatures']:
                shutil.copytree(REPO/path,context/'source'/path,ignore=shutil.ignore_patterns('*.o','*.d','owlbeard'))
            for path in ['scripts/research-artifact.py','tests/experiment_fixture.c']:
                target=context/'source'/path
                target.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
                shutil.copy2(REPO/path,target)
            shutil.copytree(HERE/'integration',context/'source/integration')
            shutil.copy2(HERE/'integration/Dockerfile',context/'Dockerfile')
            guest_image=image+'-guest'
            run(['docker','build','--build-arg','PYTHON_IMAGE='+BASE,'--tag',guest_image,context])
            result=output(['docker','run','--rm','--init','--network','none','--user','0:0',
                '--cap-add','SYS_PTRACE','--security-opt','apparmor=unconfined','--security-opt','systempaths=unconfined',
                '--tmpfs','/sys:rw',guest_image])
            # Keep guest compiler output in logs; receipt is the final JSON line.
            receipt=json.loads(result.splitlines()[-1])
            write(destination/'guest-integration.json',receipt)
        write(destination/'image.json',dict(schema='owlbear.bigrae.worker/v1',image_digest=digest,
            image_id=image_id,architecture=output(['docker','image','inspect',image,'--format','{{.Architecture}}']),base_image=BASE,sdk=sdk,worker_archive_sha256=sha(destination/'worker.tar'),
            integration_only=True,guest_integration=guest))
        shutil.copy2(HERE/'sdk.json',destination/'sdk.json')
        shutil.copy2(wheelhouse/'requirements.lock',destination/'requirements.lock')
        (destination/'SHA256SUMS').write_text(''.join(f'{sha(p)}  {p.name}\n' for p in sorted(destination.iterdir())
                                                  if p.is_file() and p.name != 'Dockerfile'))
        print('Verified worker release:',destination)
    finally:
        run(['docker','rm','--force',registry],stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--output',type=Path,required=True,help='new release directory')
    parser.add_argument('--guest-integration',action='store_true',help='ARM64 fake-kernel guest test; never a live tenant')
    args=parser.parse_args()
    build(args.output,args.guest_integration)

```

`adapters/bigrae/src/owlbear_bigrae/__init__.py`:

```py
"""Independent BigRAE runtime adapter; no platform or provider imports."""
import argparse
import base64
from importlib.resources import files
import json
import re

from bigrae_adapter_sdk.runtime import GuestAction, PluginManifest, RuntimePlan, decode_message

PARAMETERS = ["configuration"]
VERSION = "0.1.0"


def configuration(raw):
    value = decode_message(raw, limit=8192)
    keys = {"release", "manifest_sha256", "build_id", "version", "source_commit",
            "kernel_release", "target_pid", "sensors", "enforce"}
    if not isinstance(value, dict) or set(value) != keys:
        raise ValueError("configuration keys must exactly match the adapter contract")
    for key in ("manifest_sha256", "build_id"):
        if not isinstance(value[key], str) or not re.fullmatch(r"[a-f0-9]{64}", value[key]):
            raise ValueError("exact SHA-256 identities required")
    patterns = {"release": r"/opt/owlbear/releases/[a-zA-Z0-9._-]+",
                "version": r"[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+", "source_commit": r"[a-f0-9]{40}",
                "kernel_release": r"[a-zA-Z0-9._+-]{1,128}"}
    for key, pattern in patterns.items():
        if not isinstance(value[key], str) or not re.fullmatch(pattern, value[key]):
            raise ValueError("invalid release identity")
    if value["release"].rsplit("/", 1)[-1] in {".", ".."}:
        raise ValueError("invalid release path")
    if type(value["target_pid"]) is not int or not 1 < value["target_pid"] <= 2147483647:
        raise ValueError("target must be an explicit process PID")
    if type(value["enforce"]) is not bool:
        raise ValueError("enforce must be boolean")
    sensors = value["sensors"]
    if (not isinstance(sensors, list) or any(s not in ("lsm", "trace", "kprobe", "net") for s in sensors)
            or len(sensors) != len(set(sensors))):
        raise ValueError("invalid sensor selection")
    return value


def manifest(worker_image):
    return PluginManifest(protocol="bigrae.runtime-plugin/v1", plugin_id="owlbear",
                          version=VERSION, distribution="owlbear-bigrae-adapter", entry_point="owlbear",
                          worker_image=worker_image, capabilities=["guest.configure", "guest.verify"],
                          required_bindings=["guest"], required_parameters=PARAMETERS)


class Plugin:
    def plan(self, request):
        try:
            expected = manifest(request.manifest.worker_image)
            if request.manifest != expected or request.targets["guest"].os_family != "linux":
                raise ValueError("unsupported installed declaration or guest")
            config = configuration(request.parameters["configuration"])
            actions = []
            if request.phase in ("configure", "verify"):
                payload = dict(configuration=config, phase=request.phase, operation=str(request.operation_id))
                data = base64.b64encode(json.dumps(payload).encode()).decode()
                source = files(__package__).joinpath("guest.py").read_text()
                script = "python3 - <<'OWLB_GUEST'\n" + source + f"\nrun('{data}')\nOWLB_GUEST\n"
                actions = [GuestAction(action_id="owlbear-" + request.phase, binding="guest",
                                       script=script, timeout_seconds=120)]
            # Core destroys the guest; cleanup must not stop another operation's process.
            result = RuntimePlan(protocol=request.protocol, invocation_id=request.invocation_id,
                                 input_digest=request.digest, phase=request.phase, status="planned", actions=actions)
            result.authorize(request)
            return result
        except (ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
            return RuntimePlan(protocol=request.protocol, invocation_id=request.invocation_id,
                               input_digest=request.digest, phase=request.phase, status="failed",
                               failure_code="invalid-input")


def factory():
    return Plugin()


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Emit the exact BigRAE installation manifest")
    parser.add_argument("--worker-image", required=True, help="registry image@sha256:digest")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    print(manifest(args.worker_image).model_dump_json(indent=2))

```

`adapters/bigrae/src/owlbear_bigrae/guest.py`:

```py
"""Standard-library guest action, embedded in the SDK plan; never executed by planning."""
import base64
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import platform
import socket
import stat
import struct
import subprocess
import time


def command(args, timeout=20):
    return subprocess.check_output(args, text=True, timeout=timeout, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL).strip()


def release_identity(config):
    root = Path(config["release"])
    if root.resolve(strict=True) != root or root.is_symlink():
        raise ValueError("release path must be canonical")
    for item in (root, *root.parents, *root.rglob("*")):
        info = item.lstat()
        if item.is_symlink() or info.st_uid != 0 or info.st_mode & 0o022:
            raise ValueError("release and ancestors must be root-owned and not writable by others")
    raw = (root / "manifest.json").read_bytes()
    if hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest() != config["manifest_sha256"]:
        raise ValueError("manifest identity mismatch")
    manifest = json.loads(raw)
    # Authenticate the verifier before executing it. It then verifies the entire payload.
    verifier = root / "scripts/research-artifact.py"
    if verifier.is_symlink() or hashlib.sha256(verifier.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != manifest["files"]["scripts/research-artifact.py"]["sha256"]:
        raise ValueError("artifact verifier mismatch")
    identity = json.loads(command(["python3", str(verifier), "verify", str(root)], timeout=45))
    for key in ("build_id", "version", "source_commit", "kernel_release"):
        if identity[key] != config[key]:
            raise ValueError("release identity mismatch")
    if identity["architecture"] != "arm64" or platform.machine() != "aarch64" or platform.release() != config["kernel_release"]:
        raise ValueError("guest architecture or kernel mismatch")
    if json.loads(command([str(root / "daemon/owlbeard"), "--version"])) != identity:
        raise ValueError("daemon identity mismatch")
    return root, identity


def module_note_id(note):
    namesz, descsz, kind = struct.unpack_from("<III", note)
    offset = 12 + ((namesz + 3) & ~3)
    if kind != 3 or note[12:12+namesz] != b"GNU\0" or descsz == 0 or len(note) < offset + descsz:
        raise ValueError("invalid loaded module identity note")
    return note[offset:offset+descsz].hex()


def loaded_module_matches(root):
    # Compare the loaded GNU build note with the authenticated ELF module's note.
    build_id = module_note_id(Path("/sys/module/owlbear/notes/.note.gnu.build-id").read_bytes())
    expected = command(["readelf", "-n", str(root / "kernel/owlbear.ko")])
    if not any(line.strip() == "Build ID: " + build_id
               for line in expected.splitlines()):
        raise ValueError("loaded module differs from selected release")


def health(path):
    with socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX, socket.SOCK_SEQPACKET) as client:
        client.settimeout(2)
        client.connect(str(path))
        client.sendall(b"owlbear.control.v1 health")
        raw, _, flags, _ = client.recvmsg(8192)
        if flags & socket.MSG_TRUNC:
            raise ValueError("oversized health reply")
        return json.loads(raw)


def verify_health(state, config):
    mask = sum({"lsm": 1, "trace": 2, "kprobe": 4, "net": 8}[s] for s in config["sensors"])
    for key in ("ok", "ready", "kernel_responsive", "bpf_configured", "bpf_delivery",
                "code_integrity_baseline", "vdso_baseline", "signatures_loaded"):
        if state.get(key) is not True:
            raise ValueError("guest readiness condition not realized: " + key)
    for key in ("pid", "target_pid", "kernel_target_pid", "attached_bpf", "requested_bpf"):
        if type(state.get(key)) is not int:
            raise ValueError("invalid health integer: " + key)
    if state["pid"] <= 1:
        raise ValueError("invalid daemon PID")
    if (state.get("schema") != "owlbear.control.v1" or state.get("output_failed") is not False
            or state.get("target_pid") != config["target_pid"] or state.get("kernel_target_pid") != config["target_pid"]
            or state.get("attached_bpf") != mask or state.get("requested_bpf") != mask
            or state.get("kernel_enforce") is not config["enforce"]
            or state.get("requested_enforce") is not config["enforce"]
            or state.get("lsm_enforce") is not (config["enforce"] if mask & 1 else None)
            or type(state.get("kernel_dropped")) is not int or state["kernel_dropped"] != 0):
        raise ValueError("guest runtime configuration mismatch or known evidence loss")
    return state


def execute(payload):
    config = payload["configuration"]
    root, identity = release_identity(config)
    run_dir = Path("/run/owlbear-" + payload["operation"])
    configured = payload["phase"] == "configure"
    if configured:
        run_dir.mkdir(mode=0o700, exist_ok=True)
    permissions = run_dir.lstat()
    if not stat.S_ISDIR(permissions.st_mode) or permissions.st_uid != 0 or stat.S_IMODE(permissions.st_mode) != 0o700:
        raise ValueError("private root-owned runtime directory required")
    control = run_dir / "control.sock"
    if configured and not control.exists():
        if Path("/sys/module/owlbear").exists():
            loaded_module_matches(root)
        else:
            command(["insmod", str(root / "kernel/owlbear.ko"), f"target_pid={config['target_pid']}"])
        args = [str(root / "daemon/owlbeard"), "--target", str(config["target_pid"]),
                "--sensors", ",".join(config["sensors"]) or "none", "--control", str(control),
                "--events", str(run_dir / "events.jsonl"), "--sigs", str(root / "signatures/default.sigs")]
        if config["enforce"]:
            args.append("--enforce")
        with (run_dir / "daemon.log").open("xb") as log:
            subprocess.Popen(args, stdout=log, stderr=log, start_new_session=True, close_fds=True)
    loaded_module_matches(root)
    deadline = time.monotonic() + 15
    while True:
        try:
            state = verify_health(health(control), config)
            if Path(f"/proc/{state['pid']}/exe").resolve(strict=True) != root / "daemon/owlbeard":
                raise ValueError("running daemon identity mismatch")
            break
        except (OSError, ValueError):
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                raise
            time.sleep(.25)
    receipt = {"schema": "owlbear.bigrae.readiness/v1", "build": identity, "health": state}
    print(json.dumps(receipt, separators=(",", ":")))


def run(encoded):
    try:
        if os.geteuid() != 0:
            raise ValueError("root guest execution required")
        execute(json.loads(base64.b64decode(encoded, validate=True)))
    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError, struct.error, subprocess.SubprocessError):
        raise SystemExit("owlbear guest configuration/readback failed") from None

```

`adapters/bigrae/tests/test_adapter.py`:

```py
"""Installed-wheel tests; subprocess worker tests are local integration tests."""
import json
import os
import subprocess
import socket
import struct
import threading
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from uuid import uuid4

from bigrae_adapter_sdk.runtime import RuntimeInput, parse_input, parse_result
from owlbear_bigrae import configuration, factory, manifest
from owlbear_bigrae.guest import verify_health, module_note_id, health

IMAGE = "registry.example.org/owlbear/worker@sha256:" + "a" * 64
CONFIG = dict(release="/opt/owlbear/releases/release-1", manifest_sha256="b" * 64,
              build_id="c" * 64, source_commit="d" * 40, version="2.5.0",
              kernel_release="6.17.0-test", target_pid=123, sensors=["lsm", "trace"], enforce=True)


def request(phase="verify"):
    return RuntimeInput(protocol="bigrae.runtime-plugin/v1", invocation_id=uuid4(), operation_id=uuid4(),
                        pack_digest="sha256:" + "e" * 64, manifest=manifest(IMAGE), phase=phase,
                        provider="aws", range_id=1, targets={"guest": {"node_address": "nodes.guest", "os_family": "linux"}},
                        parameters={"configuration": json.dumps(CONFIG)})


def ready():
    return dict(schema="owlbear.control.v1", ok=True, ready=True, kernel_responsive=True,
                bpf_configured=True, bpf_delivery=True, code_integrity_baseline=True,
                vdso_baseline=True, signatures_loaded=True, output_failed=False,
                target_pid=123, kernel_target_pid=123, attached_bpf=3, requested_bpf=3,
                kernel_enforce=True, requested_enforce=True, lsm_enforce=True,
                kernel_dropped=0, bpf_dropped=None, evidence_loss_known=False, pid=999)


class AdapterTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_plans_bind_to_request_and_only_declared_guest(self):
        for phase in ("validate", "configure", "verify", "cleanup"):
            with self.subTest(phase=phase):
                original = request(phase)
                result = factory().plan(original)
                result.authorize(original)
                self.assertEqual(result.status, "planned")
                self.assertEqual(len(result.actions), int(phase in ("configure", "verify")))
                if result.actions:
                    self.assertEqual(result.actions[0].binding, "guest")
                    self.assertLessEqual(result.actions[0].timeout_seconds, 120)
                    self.assertIn("owlbear.control.v1 health", result.actions[0].script)
                replay = request(phase)
                with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                    parse_result(result.model_dump_json(), replay)

    def test_configuration_rejects_unsafe_and_ambiguous_values(self):
        cases = [("release", "/opt/owlbear/releases/.."), ("release", "/tmp/release;id"),
                 ("release", "/opt/owlbear/releases/../other"), ("enforce", "true"),
                 ("manifest_sha256", "latest"), ("target_pid", True), ("target_pid", 1),
                 ("sensors", ["all"]), ("sensors", ["lsm", "lsm"]), ("sensors", [None])]
        for key, value in cases:
            with self.subTest(key=key, value=value):
                config = dict(CONFIG, **{key: value})
                with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                    configuration(json.dumps(config))
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            configuration(json.dumps(dict(CONFIG, cloud_password="forbidden")))
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            configuration(json.dumps(CONFIG)[:-1] + ',"enforce":false}')

    def test_health_readback_and_negative_conditions(self):
        self.assertEqual(verify_health(ready(), CONFIG), ready())
        for key, value in [("ready", False), ("attached_bpf", 1), ("requested_bpf", 15),
                           ("kernel_enforce", False), ("lsm_enforce", False), ("target_pid", 456),
                           ("kernel_target_pid", 456), ("schema", "other"), ("output_failed", True),
                           ("kernel_dropped", 1), ("kernel_dropped", False), ("code_integrity_baseline", False)]:
            with self.subTest(key=key):
                with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                    verify_health(dict(ready(), **{key: value}), CONFIG)
        observe = dict(CONFIG, enforce=False, sensors=[])
        state = dict(ready(), requested_bpf=0, attached_bpf=0, kernel_enforce=False,
                     requested_enforce=False, lsm_enforce=None)
        verify_health(state, observe)

    def test_wrong_declarations_fail_without_actions(self):
        for os_family, version in [("windows", "0.1.0"), ("linux", "9.0.0")]:
            data = request().model_dump(mode="json")
            data["targets"]["guest"]["os_family"] = os_family
            data["manifest"]["version"] = version
            result = factory().plan(RuntimeInput.model_validate(data))
            self.assertEqual(result.status, "failed")
            self.assertEqual(result.actions, [])

    def test_worker_from_installed_packages_outside_checkout(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            for phase in ("inspect", "validate", "configure", "verify"):
                data = request("verify").model_dump(mode="json")
                if phase == "inspect":
                    data = {key: data[key] for key in ("protocol", "invocation_id", "manifest")}
                data["phase"] = phase
                original = parse_input(json.dumps(data))
                environment = {k: v for k, v in os.environ.items() if k not in ("PYTHONPATH", "SHIFTER_PLUGIN_INPUT")}
                environment["BIGRAE_PLUGIN_INPUT"] = original.model_dump_json()
                proc = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-I", "-m", "bigrae_adapter_sdk.worker"],
                                      env=environment, cwd=tmp, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
                self.assertEqual(proc.returncode, 0, proc.stderr)
                result = parse_result(proc.stdout, original)
                self.assertEqual(result.status, "compatible" if phase == "inspect" else "planned")

    def test_worker_image_requires_exact_registry_digest(self):
        for image in ("registry.example.org/owlbear/worker:latest", "sha256:" + "a" * 64):
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                manifest(image)

    def test_loaded_module_note_preserves_exact_identity(self):
        note = struct.pack("<III", 4, 20, 3) + b"GNU\0" + bytes(range(20))
        self.assertEqual(module_note_id(note), bytes(range(20)).hex())
        for bad in (note[:-1], note.replace(b"GNU", b"BAD"), b"", struct.pack("<III", 4, 0, 3) + b"GNU\0"):
            with self.subTest(note=bad), self.assertRaises((ValueError, struct.error)):
                module_note_id(bad)

    def test_health_socket_integration_and_truncation(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            path = tmp + "/control.sock"
            with socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX, socket.SOCK_SEQPACKET) as server:
                server.bind(path)
                server.listen(2)
                server.settimeout(3)
                received = []
                def serve():
                    for reply in (json.dumps(ready()).encode(), b"x" * 9000):
                        connection, _ = server.accept()
                        with connection:
                            connection.settimeout(3)
                            received.append(connection.recv(100))
                            connection.sendall(reply)
                thread = threading.Thread(target=serve, daemon=True)
                thread.start()
                self.assertEqual(health(path), ready())
                with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                    health(path)
                thread.join(timeout=4)
                self.assertFalse(thread.is_alive())
                self.assertEqual(received, [b"owlbear.control.v1 health"] * 2)
